It's particularly useful for structured document processing like invoices, forms, and reports.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install pdfplumber\n```\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Opening and Reading a PDF\n```python\nimport pdfplumber\n\n# Open a PDF file\nwith pdfplumber.open('document.pdf') as pdf:\n    # Get number of pages\n    num_pages = len(pdf.pages)\n\n    # Iterate through pages (0-indexed)\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        # Extract text from the page\n        text = page.extract_text()\n        print(text)\n```\n\n### Page-by-Page Processing\n```python\nimport pdfplumber\n\nwith pdfplumber.open('invoices.pdf') as pdf:\n    for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):\n        text = page.extract_text()\n        print(f\"Page {page_num}:\")\n        print(text)\n        print(\"-\" * 50)\n```\n\n### Extracting Tables\n```python\nwith pdfplumber.open('document.pdf') as pdf:\n    page = pdf.pages[0]\n\n    # Extract tables from the page\n    tables = page.extract_tables()\n\n    for table in tables:\n        for row in table:\n            print(row)\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for Invoice Processing\n\n### Extracting Specific Fields with Regex\n```python\nimport re\nimport pdfplumber\n\ndef extract_invoice_data(page_text):\n    \"\"\"Extract structured data from invoice text.\"\"\"\n    data = {}\n\n    # Extract vendor name (example pattern)\n    vendor_match = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+)', page_text)\n    if vendor_match:\n        data['vendor'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n\n    # Extract amount\n    amount_match = re.search(r'Total:\\s*\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', page_text)\n    if amount_match:\n        amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n        data['amount'] = float(amount_str)\n\n    # Extract PO number\n    po_match = re.search(r'PO[- ]?#?:?\\s*(PO-\\d+)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if po_match:\n        data['po_number'] = po_match.group(1)\n\n    # Extract IBAN\n    iban_match = re.search(r'IBAN:\\s*([A-Z0-9]+)', page_text)\n    if iban_match:\n        data['iban'] = iban_match.group(1)\n\n    return data\n\n# Usage\nwith pdfplumber.open('invoices.pdf') as pdf:\n    for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):\n        text = page.extract_text()\n        invoice_data = extract_invoice_data(text)\n        invoice_data['page_number'] = page_num\n        print(invoice_data)\n```\n\n## Key Features\n- **Text extraction**: Get all text from a page\n- **Table extraction**: Automatically detect and extract tables\n- **Metadata access**: Get PDF metadata like author, creation date\n- **Page properties**: Access page dimensions, rotation, etc.\n\n## Best Practices\n1. Always use context managers (`with` statement) to properly close PDF files\n2. Handle missing or malformed data gracefully with try-except blocks\n3. Use regex patterns that are flexible enough to handle variations in formatting\n4. Test patterns on sample data before processing large batches\n5. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01VnmvohUW9bT9JQNp3haU9g","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01NJS6ez6cxNhVPZ22isNRF8","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/fuzzy-string-matching.md","content":"# Fuzzy String Matching with RapidFuzz\n\n## Overview\nRapidFuzz is a fast string matching library that helps find similar strings even when they have typos, different formatting, or minor variations. It's useful for matching vendor names, addresses, or any text that might have inconsistencies.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Simple Ratio\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\n# Calculate similarity ratio (0-100)\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"Acme Corp\", \"Acme Corporation\")\nprint(score)  # High score indicates similarity\n```\n\n### Different Matching Algorithms\n\n#### Ratio (Levenshtein Distance)\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\n# Basic character-by-character comparison\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"hello world\", \"hello world!\")\n# 95.65 - very similar\n```\n\n#### Partial Ratio\n```python\n# Matches substrings (useful when one string is contained in another)\nscore = fuzz.partial_ratio(\"hello world\", \"world\")\n# 100 - \"world\" is fully contained\n```\n\n#### Token Sort Ratio\n```python\n# Ignores word order\nscore = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(\"Acme Corp Ltd\", \"Ltd Acme Corp\")\n# 100 - same words, different order\n```\n\n#### Token Set Ratio\n```python\n# Ignores word order and duplicate words\nscore = fuzz.token_set_ratio(\"Acme Corp Ltd Ltd\", \"Ltd Acme Corp\")\n# 100 - same unique words\n```\n\n## Finding Best Match\n\n### Extract One (Find Single Best Match)\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process, fuzz\n\n# List of approved vendors\nvendors = [\"Acme Corporation\", \"Beta Industries\", \"Gamma Limited\"]\n\n# Find best match for a query\nquery = \"Acme Corp\"\nresult = process.extractOne(query, vendors, scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio)\n\nif result:\n    best_match, score, index = result\n    print(f\"Best match: {best_match} (score: {score})\")\n\n    # Use threshold to determine if match is good enough\n    if score > 80:\n        print(\"Good match!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"No good match found\")\n```\n\n### Extract (Find Multiple Matches)\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process\n\n# Find top 3 matches\nquery = \"Acme\"\nresults = process.extract(query, vendors, limit=3, scorer=fuzz.ratio)\n\nfor match, score, index in results:\n    print(f\"{match}: {score}\")\n```\n\n## Practical Examples\n\n### Vendor Name Matching with Threshold\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process, fuzz\n\ndef find_vendor(invoice_vendor, approved_vendors, threshold=85):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor from approved list.\n\n    Args:\n        invoice_vendor: Vendor name from invoice\n        approved_vendors: List of approved vendor names\n        threshold: Minimum score to consider a match (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        Matched vendor name or None\n    \"\"\"\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        invoice_vendor,\n        approved_vendors,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        return result[0]  # Return matched vendor name\n    return None\n\n# Usage\napproved = [\"Acme Corporation Ltd\", \"Beta Industries Inc\", \"Gamma Limited\"]\ninvoice_name = \"Acme Corp\"\n\nmatch = find_vendor(invoice_name, approved)\nif match:\n    print(f\"Matched: {match}\")\nelse:\n    print(\"No match found - unknown vendor\")\n```\n\n### Matching with Data Structures\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process, fuzz\n\n# When you have vendor objects with multiple fields\nvendors_dict = {\n    \"Acme Corporation Ltd\": {\"id\": \"V001\", \"iban\": \"US1234567890\"},\n    \"Beta Industries Inc\": {\"id\": \"V002\", \"iban\": \"GB0987654321\"},\n    \"Gamma Limited\": {\"id\": \"V003\", \"iban\": \"DE5555555555\"}\n}\n\ndef find_vendor_info(invoice_vendor, vendors_dict, threshold=85):\n    \"\"\"Find vendor and return their info.\"\"\"\n    vendor_names = list(vendors_dict.keys())\n\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        invoice_vendor,\n        vendor_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        matched_name = result[0]\n        return vendors_dict[matched_name]\n\n    return None\n\n# Usage\nvendor_info = find_vendor_info(\"Acme Corp\", vendors_dict)\nif vendor_info:\n    print(f\"Vendor ID: {vendor_info['id']}\")\n    print(f\"IBAN: {vendor_info['iban']}\")\n```\n\n## Choosing the Right Scorer\n\n| Scorer | Use Case | Example |\n|--------|----------|---------|\n| `fuzz.ratio` | Exact character matching | \"ABC123\" vs \"ABC124\" |\n| `fuzz.partial_ratio` | Substring matching | \"Microsoft Corp\" vs \"Corp\" |\n| `fuzz.token_sort_ratio` | Ignoring word order | \"Acme Corp Ltd\" vs \"Ltd Acme Corp\" |\n| `fuzz.token_set_ratio` | Word set comparison | \"The Acme Corp\" vs \"Acme Corp\" |\n\n### Recommended for Vendor Names\nFor vendor names with variations like \"Ltd\", \"Inc\", \"Corporation\", use:\n```python\nscorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio  # Handles word order and common variations\n```\n\n## Threshold Guidelines\n\n- **90-100**: Very close match (minor typos or abbreviations)\n- **80-89**: Good match (some variations, different formatting)\n- **70-79**: Partial match (significant differences)\n- **Below 70**: Likely different entities\n\nFor vendor validation, typically use threshold of **80-85** to balance between catching variations and avoiding false positives.\n\n## Performance Optimization\n\n### Pre-processing for Better Matches\n```python\ndef normalize_vendor_name(name):\n    \"\"\"Normalize vendor names for better matching.\"\"\"\n    import re\n\n    # Convert to lowercase\n    name = name.lower()\n\n    # Remove common suffixes\n    name = re.sub(r'\\b(ltd|inc|corp|llc|limited|incorporated)\\b\\.?', '', name)\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    name = ' '.join(name.split())\n\n    return name.strip()\n\n# Usage\nquery = normalize_vendor_name(\"Acme Corp Ltd.\")\nchoices = [normalize_vendor_name(v) for v in approved_vendors]\n```\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n- Using `fuzz.ratio` when word order differences exist\n- Setting threshold too high (missing valid variations) or too low (false positives)\n- Not handling empty strings or None values\n- Forgetting that scores are 0-100, not 0-1\n- Not normalizing data before comparison (case sensitivity, extra spaces)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1349,"cache_read_input_tokens":45918,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1349,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a39084e9-662e-4b67-8cc7-9c3af82e5910","uuid":"91326ce2-a400-4a46-a268-3f5d9f9f3389"}

It's useful for matching vendor names, addresses, or any text that might have inconsistencies.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Simple Ratio\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\n# Calculate similarity ratio (0-100)\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"Acme Corp\", \"Acme Corporation\")\nprint(score)  # 'invoice_page_number',\n        'vendor_name',\n        'invoice_amount',\n        'iban',\n        'po_number',\n        'reason'\n    ]\n\n    if not isinstance(data, list):\n        raise context managers** (`with` statement) for file operations\n2. **Use `indent` parameter** for human-readable output\n3. **Use `.get()` method** for optional fields to avoid KeyError\n4. **Handle None 2)\n}\n```\n\n## Converting Between JSON and Python Types\n\n### JSON to Python\n- `object` → `dict`\n- `array` → `list`\n- `string` → `str`\n- `number (int)` → `int`\n- `number (real)` → `float`\n- context managers** (`with` statement) for file operations\n2. **Use `indent` parameter** for human-readable output\n3. **Use `.get()` method** for optional fields to avoid KeyError\n4. **Handle None Python's `re` module provides powerful regex capabilities for text processing.\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Importing\n```python\nimport re\n```\n\n### Common Functions\n\n#### `re.search()` - Find First Match\n```python\nimport re\n\ntext = \"Invoice total: $1,234.56\"\n\n# Find first occurrence\nmatch = re.search(r'\\$[\\d,]+\\.?\\d*', text)\nif match:\n    print(match.group())  # $1,234.56\n```\n\n#### `re.findall()` - Find All Matches\n```python\ntext = \"Amounts: $100, $200, $300\"\n\n# Find all matches\namounts = re.findall(r'\\$\\d+', text)\nprint(amounts)  # ['$100', '$200', '$300']\n```\n\n#### `re.match()` - Match at Start\n```python\ntext = \"PO-1234 is the order number\"\n\n# Only matches if pattern is at the start\nmatch = re.match(r'PO-\\d+', text)\nif match:\n    print(match.group())  # PO-1234\n```\n\n#### `re.sub()` - Replace Pattern\n```python\ntext = \"Phone: (123) 456-7890\"\n\n# Replace pattern\nclean = re.sub(r'[()-\\s]', '', text)\nprint(clean)  # Phone:1234567890\n```\n\n## Pattern Syntax\n\n### Basic Patterns\n- `.` - Any character except newline\n- `\\d` - Any digit (0-9)\n- `\\w` - Any word character (a-z, A-Z, 0-9, _)\n- `\\s` - Any whitespace\n- `\\D` - Not a digit\n- `\\W` - Not a word character\n- `\\S` - Not whitespace\n\n### Quantifiers\n- `*` - 0 or more times\n- `+` - 1 or more times\n- `?` - 0 or 1 time (optional)\n- `{n}` - Exactly n times\n- `{n,}` - n or more times\n- `{n,m}` - Between n and m times\n\n### Character Classes\n- `[abc]` - Match a, b, or c\n- `[a-z]` - Match any lowercase letter\n- `[A-Z]` - Match any uppercase letter\n- `[0-9]` - Match any digit\n- `[^abc]` - Match anything except a, b, or c\n\n### Anchors\n- `^` - Start of string/line\n- `$` - End of string/line\n- `\\b` - Word boundary\n\n### Groups\n- `(...)` - Capturing group\n- `(?:...)` - Non-capturing group\n- `(a|b)` - Match a or b\n\n## Practical Examples for Invoice Processing\n\n### Extract Vendor Name\n```python\nimport re\n\ntext = \"\"\"\nInvoice #12345\nVendor: Acme Corporation Ltd\nDate: 2024-01-15\n\"\"\"\n\n# Extract vendor name\nmatch = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+)', text)\nif match:\n    vendor = match.group(1).strip()\n    print(vendor)  # Acme Corporation Ltd\n```\n\n### Extract Amount with Currency\n```python\ntext = \"Total Amount: $5,432.10\"\n\n# Extract numeric amount\nmatch = re.search(r'\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', text)\nif match:\n    amount_str = match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n    amount = float(amount_str)\n    print(amount)  # 5432.10\n```\n\n### Extract PO Number (Multiple Formats)\n```python\ntext = \"PO Number: PO-1234\"\n\n# Flexible pattern for different formats\n# Matches: PO-1234, PO 1234, PO1234, po-1234\nmatch = re.search(r'PO[- ]?#?:?\\s*(PO-?\\d+)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\nif match:\n    po_number = match.group(1)\n    print(po_number)  # PO-1234\n```\n\n### Extract IBAN\n```python\ntext = \"Bank Account IBAN: GB82WEST12345698765432\"\n\n# IBAN pattern (2 letters + alphanumeric)\nmatch = re.search(r'IBAN:?\\s*([A-Z]{2}[A-Z0-9]+)', text)\nif match:\n    iban = match.group(1)\n    print(iban)  # GB82WEST12345698765432\n```\n\n### Extract Date\n```python\ntext = \"Invoice Date: 2024-01-15\"\n\n# Date pattern (YYYY-MM-DD)\nmatch = re.search(r'(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})', text)\nif match:\n    date = match.group(1)\n    print(date)  # 2024-01-15\n```\n\n## Advanced Patterns\n\n### Using Named Groups\n```python\nimport re\n\ntext = \"Invoice: INV-001, Amount: $1,234.56, PO: PO-5678\"\n\n# Named groups for clarity\npattern = r'Invoice:\\s*(?P<invoice>[A-Z]+-\\d+).*Amount:\\s*\\$(?P<amount>[\\d,]+\\.?\\d*).*PO:\\s*(?P<po>PO-\\d+)'\n\nmatch = re.search(pattern, text)\nif match:\n    print(match.group('invoice'))  # INV-001\n    print(match.group('amount'))   # 1,234.56\n    print(match.group('po'))       # PO-5678\n\n    # Or access as dictionary\n    data = match.groupdict()\n    print(data)  # {'invoice': 'INV-001', 'amount': '1,234.56', 'po': 'PO-5678'}\n```\n\n### Case-Insensitive Matching\n```python\nimport re\n\ntext = \"vendor: ACME Corp\"\n\n# Case-insensitive flag\nmatch = re.search(r'vendor:\\s*(.+)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\nif match:\n    print(match.group(1))  # ACME Corp\n```\n\n### Multiline Matching\n```python\nimport re\n\ntext = \"\"\"\nFirst line\nSecond line with PO-1234\nThird line\n\"\"\"\n\n# Match across lines\nmatch = re.search(r'Second.*PO-\\d+', text, re.DOTALL)\nif match:\n    print(match.group())\n```\n\n## Helper Functions for Invoice Data\n\n### Complete Invoice Parser\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef extract_invoice_data(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract structured data from invoice text.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict with invoice fields or empty dict if parsing fails\n    \"\"\"\n    data = {}\n\n    # Vendor name (after \"Vendor:\" label)\n    vendor_match = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n|$)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if vendor_match:\n        data['vendor_name'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n\n    # Amount (handles $, commas, optional cents)\n    amount_match = re.search(r'(?:Total|Amount):\\s*\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if amount_match:\n        amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n        data['invoice_amount'] = float(amount_str)\n\n    # PO Number (flexible format)\n    po_match = re.search(r'PO[- ]?(?:Number|#)?:?\\s*(PO-\\d+)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if po_match:\n        data['po_number'] = po_match.group(1).upper()\n\n    # IBAN\n    iban_match = re.search(r'IBAN:?\\s*([A-Z]{2}[A-Z0-9]+)', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if iban_match:\n        data['iban'] = iban_match.group(1).upper()\n\n    return data\n\n# Usage\ninvoice_text = \"\"\"\nInvoice #12345\nVendor: Acme Corp Ltd\nTotal: $5,432.10\nPO Number: PO-1001\nIBAN: US1234567890\n\"\"\"\n\ndata = extract_invoice_data(invoice_text)\nprint(data)\n```\n\n## Common Regex Patterns for Financial Documents\n\n| Pattern | Regex | Example Match |\n|---------|-------|---------------|\n| Currency Amount `[A-Z]{2}[A-Z0-9]{13,}` | GB82WEST12345 |\n| Email | `[\\w.-]+@[\\w.-]+\\.\\w+` | user@example.com |\n| Phone | `\\(?\\d{3}\\)?[-.\\s]?\\d{3}[-.\\s]?\\d{4}` | (123) 456-7890 |\n\n## Best Extract vendor name (after \"Vendor:\" label)\n    vendor_match = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n|$)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if vendor_match:\n        data['vendor_name'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n    else:\n        data['vendor_name'] = None\n\n    # Extract amount (handles $, commas, optional cents)\n    amount_match = re.search(r'(?:Total|Amount):\\s*\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if amount_match:\n        amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n        data['invoice_amount'] = float(amount_str)\n    else:\n        data['invoice_amount'] = None\n\n    # Extract PO Number (flexible format, may not exist)\n    po_match = re.search(r'PO[- ]?(?:Number|#)?:?\\s*(PO-\\d+)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if po_match:\n        data['po_number'] = po_match.group(1).upper()\n    else:\n        data['po_number'] = None\n\n    # Extract IBAN\n    iban_match = re.search(r'IBAN:?\\s*([A-Z0-9]+)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if iban_match:\n        data['iban'] = iban_match.group(1).upper()\n    else:\n        data['iban'] = None\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef load_vendors(filepath):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load approved vendors from Excel file.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict mapping vendor names to their info (vendor_id, iban)\n    \"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_excel(filepath)\n\n    # Create mapping from vendor name to vendor info\n    vendors = {}\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        vendors[row['Name']] = {\n            'vendor_id': row['Vendor ID'],\n            'iban': row['IBAN']\n        }\n\n    return vendors\n\n\ndef load_purchase_orders(filepath):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load purchase orders from CSV file.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict mapping PO number to PO info (vendor_id, amount)\n    \"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_csv(filepath)\n\n    # Create mapping from PO number to PO info\n    pos = {}\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        pos[row['po_number']] = {\n            'vendor_id': row['vendor_id'],\n            'amount': float(row['amount'])\n        }\n\n    return pos\n\n\ndef find_vendor_match(invoice_vendor, approved_vendors, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        invoice_vendor: Vendor name from invoice\n        approved_vendors: Dict of approved vendor names to info\n        threshold: Minimum similarity score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        tuple of (matched_vendor_name, vendor_info) or (None, None)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None, None\n\n    vendor_names = list(approved_vendors.keys())\n\n    # Use token_sort_ratio to handle word order variations and common suffixes\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        invoice_vendor,\n        vendor_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        matched_name = result[0]\n        return matched_name, approved_vendors[matched_name]\n\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef check_fraud(invoice, approved_vendors, purchase_orders):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if an invoice is fraudulent.\n\n    Returns:\n        str with fraud reason or None if valid\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check 1: Unknown Vendor (with fuzzy matching)\n    matched_vendor_name, vendor_info = find_vendor_match(\n        invoice['vendor_name'],\n        approved_vendors,\n        threshold=80\n    )\n\n    if not vendor_info:\n        return \"Unknown Vendor\"\n\n    # Check 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if invoice['iban'] and vendor_info['iban'] != invoice['iban']:\n        return \"IBAN Mismatch\"\n\n    # Check 3: Invalid PO\n    if invoice['po_number'] and invoice['po_number'] not in purchase_orders:\n        return \"Invalid PO\"\n\n    # If PO exists, do additional checks\n    if invoice['po_number']:\n        po_info = purchase_orders[invoice['po_number']]\n\n        # Check 4: Amount Mismatch (tolerance of 0.01)\n        if abs(invoice['invoice_amount'] - po_info['amount']) > 0.01:\n            return \"Amount Mismatch\"\n\n        # Check 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if vendor_info['vendor_id'] != po_info['vendor_id']:\n            return \"Vendor Mismatch\"\n\n    # All checks passed\n    return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to detect invoice fraud.\"\"\"\n\n    # Load reference data\n    print(\"Loading vendors and purchase orders...\")\n    approved_vendors = load_vendors('/root/vendors.xlsx')\n    purchase_orders = load_purchase_orders('/root/purchase_orders.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(approved_vendors)} approved vendors\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(purchase_orders)} purchase orders\")\n\n    # Extract invoices from PDF\n    print(\"\\nExtracting invoices from PDF...\")\n    invoices = []\n\n    with pdfplumber.open('/root/invoices.pdf') as pdf:\n        for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):\n            text = page.extract_text()\n            invoice_data = extract_invoice_data(text, page_num)\n            invoices.append(invoice_data)\n\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    # Check for fraud\n    print(\"\\nChecking for fraudulent invoices...\")\n    fraudulent_invoices = []\n\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_reason = check_fraud(invoice, approved_vendors, purchase_orders)\n\n        if fraud_reason:\n            fraudulent_invoices.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': invoice['vendor_name'],\n                'invoice_amount': invoice['invoice_amount'],\n                'iban': invoice['iban'],\n                'po_number': invoice['po_number'],\n                'reason': fraud_reason\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraudulent_invoices)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    # Write report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating fraud report...\")\n    with open('/root/fraud_report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraudulent_invoices, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Fraud report saved to /root/fraud_report.json\")\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n=== FRAUD DETECTION SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total invoices: {len(invoices)}\")\n    print(f\"Fraudulent invoices: {len(fraudulent_invoices)}\")\n    print(f\"Valid invoices: {len(invoices) - len(fraudulent_invoices)}\")\n\n    if fraudulent_invoices:\n        print(\"\\nFraud breakdown by reason:\")\n        reason_counts = {}\n        for inv in fraudulent_invoices:\n            reason = inv['reason']\n            reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1\n\n        for reason, count in sorted(reason_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  {reason}: {count}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":261,"cache_read_input_tokens":53885,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":261,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a39084e9-662e-4b67-8cc7-9c3af82e5910","uuid":"dae7cbe5-00dc-49ab-89a6-cea92c71324a"}

Extract vendor name (after \"Vendor:\" label)\n    vendor_match = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n|$)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if vendor_match:\n        data['vendor_name'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n    else:\n        data['vendor_name'] = None\n\n    # Extract amount (handles $, commas, optional cents)\n    amount_match = re.search(r'(?:Total|Amount):\\s*\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if amount_match:\n        amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n        data['invoice_amount'] = float(amount_str)\n    else:\n        data['invoice_amount'] = None\n\n    # Extract PO Number (flexible format, may not exist)\n    po_match = re.search(r'PO[- ]?(?:Number|#)?:?\\s*(PO-\\d+)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if po_match:\n        data['po_number'] = po_match.group(1).upper()\n    else:\n        data['po_number'] = None\n\n    # Extract IBAN\n    iban_match = re.search(r'IBAN:?\\s*([A-Z0-9]+)', page_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if iban_match:\n        data['iban'] = iban_match.group(1).upper()\n    else:\n        data['iban'] = None\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef load_vendors(filepath):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load approved vendors from Excel file.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict mapping vendor names to their info (vendor_id, iban)\n    \"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_excel(filepath)\n\n    # Create mapping from vendor name to vendor info\n    vendors = {}\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        vendors[row['Name']] = {\n            'vendor_id': row['Vendor ID'],\n            'iban': row['IBAN']\n        }\n\n    return vendors\n\n\ndef load_purchase_orders(filepath):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load purchase orders from CSV file.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict mapping PO number to PO info (vendor_id, amount)\n    \"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_csv(filepath)\n\n    # Create mapping from PO number to PO info\n    pos = {}\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        pos[row['po_number']] = {\n            'vendor_id': row['vendor_id'],\n            'amount': float(row['amount'])\n        }\n\n    return pos\n\n\ndef find_vendor_match(invoice_vendor, approved_vendors, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        invoice_vendor: Vendor name from invoice\n        approved_vendors: Dict of approved vendor names to info\n        threshold: Minimum similarity score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        tuple of (matched_vendor_name, vendor_info) or (None, None)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None, None\n\n    vendor_names = list(approved_vendors.keys())\n\n    # Use token_sort_ratio to handle word order variations and common suffixes\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        invoice_vendor,\n        vendor_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        matched_name = result[0]\n        return matched_name, approved_vendors[matched_name]\n\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef check_fraud(invoice, approved_vendors, purchase_orders):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if an invoice is fraudulent.\n\n    Returns:\n        str with fraud reason or None if valid\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check 1: Unknown Vendor (with fuzzy matching)\n    matched_vendor_name, vendor_info = find_vendor_match(\n        invoice['vendor_name'],\n        approved_vendors,\n        threshold=80\n    )\n\n    if not vendor_info:\n        return \"Unknown Vendor\"\n\n    # Check 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if invoice['iban'] and vendor_info['iban'] != invoice['iban']:\n        return \"IBAN Mismatch\"\n\n    # Check 3: Invalid PO\n    if invoice['po_number'] and invoice['po_number'] not in purchase_orders:\n        return \"Invalid PO\"\n\n    # If PO exists, do additional checks\n    if invoice['po_number']:\n        po_info = purchase_orders[invoice['po_number']]\n\n        # Check 4: Amount Mismatch (tolerance of 0.01)\n        if abs(invoice['invoice_amount'] - po_info['amount']) > 0.01:\n            return \"Amount Mismatch\"\n\n        # Check 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if vendor_info['vendor_id'] != po_info['vendor_id']:\n            return \"Vendor Mismatch\"\n\n    # All checks passed\n    return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to detect invoice fraud.\"\"\"\n\n    # Load reference data\n    print(\"Loading vendors and purchase orders...\")\n    approved_vendors = load_vendors('/root/vendors.xlsx')\n    purchase_orders = load_purchase_orders('/root/purchase_orders.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(approved_vendors)} approved vendors\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(purchase_orders)} purchase orders\")\n\n    # Extract invoices from PDF\n    print(\"\\nExtracting invoices from PDF...\")\n    invoices = []\n\n    with pdfplumber.open('/root/invoices.pdf') as pdf:\n        for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):\n            text = page.extract_text()\n            invoice_data = extract_invoice_data(text, page_num)\n            invoices.append(invoice_data)\n\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    # Check for fraud\n    print(\"\\nChecking for fraudulent invoices...\")\n    fraudulent_invoices = []\n\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_reason = check_fraud(invoice, approved_vendors, purchase_orders)\n\n        if fraud_reason:\n            fraudulent_invoices.append({\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': invoice['vendor_name'],\n                'invoice_amount': invoice['invoice_amount'],\n                'iban': invoice['iban'],\n                'po_number': invoice['po_number'],\n                'reason': fraud_reason\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraudulent_invoices)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    # Create mapping from PO number to PO info\n    pos = {}\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        pos[row['po_number']] = {\n            'vendor_id': row['vendor_id'],\n            'amount': float(row['amount'])\n        }\n\n    return pos\n\n\ndef find_vendor_match(invoice_vendor, approved_vendors, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        invoice_vendor: Vendor name from invoice\n        approved_vendors: Dict of approved vendor names to info\n        threshold: Minimum similarity score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        tuple of (matched_vendor_name, vendor_info) or (None, None)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None, None\n\n    vendor_names = list(approved_vendors.keys())\n\n    # Use token_sort_ratio to handle word order variations and common suffixes\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        invoice_vendor,\n        vendor_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        matched_name = result[0]\n        return matched_name, approved_vendors[matched_name]\n\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef check_fraud(invoice, approved_vendors, purchase_orders):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if an invoice is fraudulent.\n\n    Returns:\n        str with fraud reason or None if valid\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if vendor_info['vendor_id'] != po_info['vendor_id']:\n            return \"Vendor Mismatch\"\n\n    # All checks passed\n    return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to detect invoice fraud.\"\"\"\n\n    #
